6,\n  formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Either[Int, (Either[Float, BigDecimal], String)]] {\n\n  override def tokenize(value: Either[Int, (Either[Float, BigDecimal], s\"${decimalValue.formatted(f\"%.${precision}f\")}\"\n        }\n        Token(strValue, TokenType.NUMERIC, Map(\"originalType\" -> originalType))\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Convenience methods for different Token = tokenize(Right((Left(value.toFloat), \"Double\")))\n  def tokenizeBigDecimal(value: BigDecimal): Token = tokenize(Right((Right(value), \"BigDecimal\")))\n}\n\n/**\n * Tokenizer for date/time types.\n */\nclass TemporalTokenizer(formatStr: Option[String] = None) extends BaseTokenizer[Either[java.time.LocalDateTime, java.time.LocalDate]] {\n\n  val IsoFormat = \"yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss\"\n }\n}\n\n/**\n * Registry with complex nested generic types.\n */\nclass TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val _registry: mutable.Map[String, TokenContainer[T]] = mutable.Map.empty\n  private val case class JsonBoolean(value: Boolean) extends JsonValue\n  case class JsonNull() extends JsonValue\n  case class JsonArray(items: List[JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\n  case class JsonObject(fields: Map[String, JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\n}\n\n/**\n * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive types.\n */\nclass JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n  import JsonValue._\n\n  def tokenize(value: JsonValue): Token = {\n    val jsonStr = if (pretty) prettyPrint(value) else compactPrint(value)\n    Token(jsonStr, TokenType.STRUCTURED, Map(\"json\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Extract and tokenize a value at a JSON path.\n   */\n  def tokenizePath(value: JsonValue, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\").toList\n    val result = parts.foldLeft[Option[JsonValue]](Some(value)) { (current, part) =>\n      current.flatMap {\n        case JsonObject(fields) => fields.get(part)\n        case JsonArray(items) =>\n          if (part.forall(_.isDigit)) {\n            val idx = part.toIntOption\n            idx.flatMap(i => items.lift(i))\n          } else None\n        case _ => None\n      }\n    }\n    result.map(tokenize)\n  }\n\n  private def compactPrint(value: JsonValue): String = value match {\n    case JsonString(s) => s\"\"\"\"$s\"\"\"\"\n    case JsonNumber(n) => n.toString\n    case JsonBoolean(b) => b.toString\n    case JsonNull() => \"null\"\n    case JsonArray(items) => items.map(compactPrint).mkString(\"[\", \",\", \"]\")\n    case JsonObject(fields) =>\n      fields.map { case (k, v) => s\"\"\"\"$k\":${compactPrint(v)}\"\"\" }.mkString(\"{\", \",\", \"}\")\n  }\n\n  private def prettyPrint(value: JsonValue): String = {\n    def pretty(value: JsonValue, indent: Int): String = value match {\n      case JsonString(s) => s\"\"\"\"$s\"\"\"\"\n      case JsonNumber(n) => n.toString\n      case JsonBoolean(b) => b.toString\n      case JsonNull() => \"null\"\n      case JsonArray(items) =>\n        if (items.isEmpty) \"[]\"\n        else {\n          val indented = items.map(i => \" \" * (indent + 2) + pretty(i, indent + 2)).mkString(\"[\\n\", \",\\n\", s\"\\n${\" \" * indent}]\")\n          indented\n        }\n      case JsonObject(fields) =>\n        if (fields.isEmpty) \"{}\"\n        else {\n          val indented = fields.map { case (k, v) =>\n            s\"\"\"${\" \" * (indent + 2)}\"$k\": ${pretty(v, indent + 2)}\"\"\"\n          }.mkString(\"{\\n\", \",\\n\", s\"\\n${\" \" * indent}}\")\n          indented\n        }\n    }\n    pretty(value, 0)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n  private val punctuation = Set(',', '.', '!', '?', ';', ':', '\\'', '\"', '(', ')', '[', ']', '{', '}')\n\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    val processed = if (stripPunctuation) {\n      word.dropWhile(punctuation.contains).dropWhile(punctuation.contains).reverse.dropWhile(punctuation.contains).reverse\n    } else word\n\n    val normalized = if (lowercase) processed.toLowerCase else processed\n\n    if (normalized.length < minLength) None\n    else maxLength match {\n      case Some(max) if normalized.length > max => Some(normalized.take(max))\n      case _ => if (normalized.nonEmpty) Some(normalized) else None\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n   */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, i) =>\n      processToken(word).map(processed =>\n        Token(processed, TokenType.STRING, Map(\"position\" -> i, \"original\" -> word))\n      )\n    }.toList\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n   */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n   */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    val result = mutable.ListBuffer.empty[(String, Int, Int)]\n    var currentPos = 0\n\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").foreach { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).foreach { processed =>\n        result += ((processed, start, end))\n      }\n\n      currentPos = end\n    }\n\n    result.toList\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n   */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).length\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Fluent builder for creating tokenizers.\n */\nclass TokenizerBuilder[T] {\n  private val normalizers: mutable.ListBuffer[String => String] = mutable.ListBuffer.empty\n  private val validators: mutable.ListBuffer[T => Boolean] = mutable.ListBuffer.empty\n  private var metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    normalizers += normalizer\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withValidator(validator: T => Boolean): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    validators += validator\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withMetadata(additionalMetadata: (String, Any)*): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    metadata = metadata ++ additionalMetadata.toMap\n    this\n  }\n\n  def build: T => Token = { value: T =>\n    // Validate\n    validators.find(!_(value)).foreach { _ =>\n      throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n    }\n\n    // Convert to string\n    var strValue = value.toString\n\n    // Normalize\n    normalizers.foreach { normalizer =>\n      strValue = normalizer(strValue)\n    }\n\n    Token(strValue, TokenType.STRING, metadata)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Convenience functions for common tokenization operations.\n */\nobject Tokenizer {\n  private val universalTokenizer = new UniversalTokenizer()\n\n  /**\n   * Tokenize a single value using the universal tokenizer.\n   */\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    universalTokenizer.tokenize(value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Tokenize a batch of values.\n   */\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[Any]): List[Token] = {\n    values.map(tokenize).toList\n  }\n\n  /**\n */\n  def stringTokenizer(encoding: String = \"utf-8\")(normalizer: String => String = identity): StringTokenizer = {\n    new StringTokenizer(encoding, normalizer)\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create a }\n}\n\n/**\n * Registry with complex nested generic types.\n */\nclass TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val _registry: mutable.Map[String, TokenContainer[T]] = mutable.Map.empty\n  private val
